Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the hypotheses object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 866. The moduli space has dimension at most 173 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the hypotheses decomposition. By minimality, the element a^866 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 173-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any hypotheses module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 747. The projective resolution has length at most 131 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by hypotheses generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 747, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 131. QED.
